Surah: 37. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s037/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 37

- 37:1 وَٱلصَّٰٓفَّٰتِ صَفًّۭا
- 37:2 فَٱلزَّٰجِرَٰتِ زَجْرًۭا
- 37:3 فَٱلتَّٰلِيَٰتِ ذِكْرًا
- 37:4 إِنَّ إِلَٰهَكُمْ لَوَٰحِدٌۭ
- 37:5 رَّبُّ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَمَا بَيْنَهُمَا وَرَبُّ ٱلْمَشَٰرِقِ
- 37:6 إِنَّا زَيَّنَّا ٱلسَّمَآءَ ٱلدُّنْيَا بِزِينَةٍ ٱلْكَوَاكِبِ
- 37:7 وَحِفْظًۭا مِّن كُلِّ شَيْطَٰنٍۢ مَّارِدٍۢ
- 37:8 لَّا يَسَّمَّعُونَ إِلَى ٱلْمَلَإِ ٱلْأَعْلَىٰ وَيُقْذَفُونَ مِن كُلِّ جَانِبٍۢ
- 37:9 دُحُورًۭا ۖ وَلَهُمْ عَذَابٌۭ وَاصِبٌ
- 37:10 إِلَّا مَنْ خَطِفَ ٱلْخَطْفَةَ فَأَتْبَعَهُۥ شِهَابٌۭ ثَاقِبٌۭ
- 37:11 فَٱسْتَفْتِهِمْ أَهُمْ أَشَدُّ خَلْقًا أَم مَّنْ خَلَقْنَآ ۚ إِنَّا خَلَقْنَٰهُم مِّن طِينٍۢ لَّازِبٍۭ
- 37:12 بَلْ عَجِبْتَ وَيَسْخَرُونَ
- 37:13 وَإِذَا ذُكِّرُوا۟ لَا يَذْكُرُونَ
- 37:14 وَإِذَا رَأَوْا۟ ءَايَةًۭ يَسْتَسْخِرُونَ
- 37:15 وَقَالُوٓا۟ إِنْ هَٰذَآ إِلَّا سِحْرٌۭ مُّبِينٌ
- 37:16 أَءِذَا مِتْنَا وَكُنَّا تُرَابًۭا وَعِظَٰمًا أَءِنَّا لَمَبْعُوثُونَ
- 37:17 أَوَءَابَآؤُنَا ٱلْأَوَّلُونَ
- 37:18 قُلْ نَعَمْ وَأَنتُمْ دَٰخِرُونَ
- 37:19 فَإِنَّمَا هِىَ زَجْرَةٌۭ وَٰحِدَةٌۭ فَإِذَا هُمْ يَنظُرُونَ
- 37:20 وَقَالُوا۟ يَٰوَيْلَنَا هَٰذَا يَوْمُ ٱلدِّينِ
- 37:21 هَٰذَا يَوْمُ ٱلْفَصْلِ ٱلَّذِى كُنتُم بِهِۦ تُكَذِّبُونَ
- 37:22 ۞ ٱحْشُرُوا۟ ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوا۟ وَأَزْوَٰجَهُمْ وَمَا كَانُوا۟ يَعْبُدُونَ
- 37:23 مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ فَٱهْدُوهُمْ إِلَىٰ صِرَٰطِ ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 37:24 وَقِفُوهُمْ ۖ إِنَّهُم مَّسْـُٔولُونَ
- 37:25 مَا لَكُمْ لَا تَنَاصَرُونَ
- 37:26 بَلْ هُمُ ٱلْيَوْمَ مُسْتَسْلِمُونَ
- 37:27 وَأَقْبَلَ بَعْضُهُمْ عَلَىٰ بَعْضٍۢ يَتَسَآءَلُونَ
- 37:28 قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّكُمْ كُنتُمْ تَأْتُونَنَا عَنِ ٱلْيَمِينِ
- 37:29 قَالُوا۟ بَل لَّمْ تَكُونُوا۟ مُؤْمِنِينَ
- 37:30 وَمَا كَانَ لَنَا عَلَيْكُم مِّن سُلْطَٰنٍۭ ۖ بَلْ كُنتُمْ قَوْمًۭا طَٰغِينَ
- 37:31 فَحَقَّ عَلَيْنَا قَوْلُ رَبِّنَآ ۖ إِنَّا لَذَآئِقُونَ
- 37:32 فَأَغْوَيْنَٰكُمْ إِنَّا كُنَّا غَٰوِينَ
- 37:33 فَإِنَّهُمْ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ فِى ٱلْعَذَابِ مُشْتَرِكُونَ
- 37:34 إِنَّا كَذَٰلِكَ نَفْعَلُ بِٱلْمُجْرِمِينَ
- 37:35 إِنَّهُمْ كَانُوٓا۟ إِذَا قِيلَ لَهُمْ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا ٱللَّهُ يَسْتَكْبِرُونَ
- 37:36 وَيَقُولُونَ أَئِنَّا لَتَارِكُوٓا۟ ءَالِهَتِنَا لِشَاعِرٍۢ مَّجْنُونٍۭ
- 37:37 بَلْ جَآءَ بِٱلْحَقِّ وَصَدَّقَ ٱلْمُرْسَلِينَ
- 37:38 إِنَّكُمْ لَذَآئِقُوا۟ ٱلْعَذَابِ ٱلْأَلِيمِ
- 37:39 وَمَا تُجْزَوْنَ إِلَّا مَا كُنتُمْ تَعْمَلُونَ
- 37:40 إِلَّا عِبَادَ ٱللَّهِ ٱلْمُخْلَصِينَ
- 37:41 أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ لَهُمْ رِزْقٌۭ مَّعْلُومٌۭ
- 37:42 فَوَٰكِهُ ۖ وَهُم مُّكْرَمُونَ
- 37:43 فِى جَنَّٰتِ ٱلنَّعِيمِ
- 37:44 عَلَىٰ سُرُرٍۢ مُّتَقَٰبِلِينَ
- 37:45 يُطَافُ عَلَيْهِم بِكَأْسٍۢ مِّن مَّعِينٍۭ
- 37:46 بَيْضَآءَ لَذَّةٍۢ لِّلشَّٰرِبِينَ
- 37:47 لَا فِيهَا غَوْلٌۭ وَلَا هُمْ عَنْهَا يُنزَفُونَ
- 37:48 وَعِندَهُمْ قَٰصِرَٰتُ ٱلطَّرْفِ عِينٌۭ
- 37:49 كَأَنَّهُنَّ بَيْضٌۭ مَّكْنُونٌۭ
- 37:50 فَأَقْبَلَ بَعْضُهُمْ عَلَىٰ بَعْضٍۢ يَتَسَآءَلُونَ
- 37:51 قَالَ قَآئِلٌۭ مِّنْهُمْ إِنِّى كَانَ لِى قَرِينٌۭ
- 37:52 يَقُولُ أَءِنَّكَ لَمِنَ ٱلْمُصَدِّقِينَ
- 37:53 أَءِذَا مِتْنَا وَكُنَّا تُرَابًۭا وَعِظَٰمًا أَءِنَّا لَمَدِينُونَ
- 37:54 قَالَ هَلْ أَنتُم مُّطَّلِعُونَ
- 37:55 فَٱطَّلَعَ فَرَءَاهُ فِى سَوَآءِ ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 37:56 قَالَ تَٱللَّهِ إِن كِدتَّ لَتُرْدِينِ
- 37:57 وَلَوْلَا نِعْمَةُ رَبِّى لَكُنتُ مِنَ ٱلْمُحْضَرِينَ
- 37:58 أَفَمَا نَحْنُ بِمَيِّتِينَ
- 37:59 إِلَّا مَوْتَتَنَا ٱلْأُولَىٰ وَمَا نَحْنُ بِمُعَذَّبِينَ
- 37:60 إِنَّ هَٰذَا لَهُوَ ٱلْفَوْزُ ٱلْعَظِيمُ
- 37:61 لِمِثْلِ هَٰذَا فَلْيَعْمَلِ ٱلْعَٰمِلُونَ
- 37:62 أَذَٰلِكَ خَيْرٌۭ نُّزُلًا أَمْ شَجَرَةُ ٱلزَّقُّومِ
- 37:63 إِنَّا جَعَلْنَٰهَا فِتْنَةًۭ لِّلظَّٰلِمِينَ
- 37:64 إِنَّهَا شَجَرَةٌۭ تَخْرُجُ فِىٓ أَصْلِ ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 37:65 طَلْعُهَا كَأَنَّهُۥ رُءُوسُ ٱلشَّيَٰطِينِ
- 37:66 فَإِنَّهُمْ لَءَاكِلُونَ مِنْهَا فَمَالِـُٔونَ مِنْهَا ٱلْبُطُونَ
- 37:67 ثُمَّ إِنَّ لَهُمْ عَلَيْهَا لَشَوْبًۭا مِّنْ حَمِيمٍۢ
- 37:68 ثُمَّ إِنَّ مَرْجِعَهُمْ لَإِلَى ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 37:69 إِنَّهُمْ أَلْفَوْا۟ ءَابَآءَهُمْ ضَآلِّينَ
- 37:70 فَهُمْ عَلَىٰٓ ءَاثَٰرِهِمْ يُهْرَعُونَ
- 37:71 وَلَقَدْ ضَلَّ قَبْلَهُمْ أَكْثَرُ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ
- 37:72 وَلَقَدْ أَرْسَلْنَا فِيهِم مُّنذِرِينَ
- 37:73 فَٱنظُرْ كَيْفَ كَانَ عَٰقِبَةُ ٱلْمُنذَرِينَ
- 37:74 إِلَّا عِبَادَ ٱللَّهِ ٱلْمُخْلَصِينَ
- 37:75 وَلَقَدْ نَادَىٰنَا نُوحٌۭ فَلَنِعْمَ ٱلْمُجِيبُونَ
- 37:76 وَنَجَّيْنَٰهُ وَأَهْلَهُۥ مِنَ ٱلْكَرْبِ ٱلْعَظِيمِ
- 37:77 وَجَعَلْنَا ذُرِّيَّتَهُۥ هُمُ ٱلْبَاقِينَ
- 37:78 وَتَرَكْنَا عَلَيْهِ فِى ٱلْءَاخِرِينَ
- 37:79 سَلَٰمٌ عَلَىٰ نُوحٍۢ فِى ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 37:80 إِنَّا كَذَٰلِكَ نَجْزِى ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ
- 37:81 إِنَّهُۥ مِنْ عِبَادِنَا ٱلْمُؤْمِنِينَ
- 37:82 ثُمَّ أَغْرَقْنَا ٱلْءَاخَرِينَ
- 37:83 ۞ وَإِنَّ مِن شِيعَتِهِۦ لَإِبْرَٰهِيمَ
- 37:84 إِذْ جَآءَ رَبَّهُۥ بِقَلْبٍۢ سَلِيمٍ
- 37:85 إِذْ قَالَ لِأَبِيهِ وَقَوْمِهِۦ مَاذَا تَعْبُدُونَ
- 37:86 أَئِفْكًا ءَالِهَةًۭ دُونَ ٱللَّهِ تُرِيدُونَ
- 37:87 فَمَا ظَنُّكُم بِرَبِّ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 37:88 فَنَظَرَ نَظْرَةًۭ فِى ٱلنُّجُومِ
- 37:89 فَقَالَ إِنِّى سَقِيمٌۭ
- 37:90 فَتَوَلَّوْا۟ عَنْهُ مُدْبِرِينَ
- 37:91 فَرَاغَ إِلَىٰٓ ءَالِهَتِهِمْ فَقَالَ أَلَا تَأْكُلُونَ
- 37:92 مَا لَكُمْ لَا تَنطِقُونَ
- 37:93 فَرَاغَ عَلَيْهِمْ ضَرْبًۢا بِٱلْيَمِينِ
- 37:94 فَأَقْبَلُوٓا۟ إِلَيْهِ يَزِفُّونَ
- 37:95 قَالَ أَتَعْبُدُونَ مَا تَنْحِتُونَ
- 37:96 وَٱللَّهُ خَلَقَكُمْ وَمَا تَعْمَلُونَ
- 37:97 قَالُوا۟ ٱبْنُوا۟ لَهُۥ بُنْيَٰنًۭا فَأَلْقُوهُ فِى ٱلْجَحِيمِ
- 37:98 فَأَرَادُوا۟ بِهِۦ كَيْدًۭا فَجَعَلْنَٰهُمُ ٱلْأَسْفَلِينَ
- 37:99 وَقَالَ إِنِّى ذَاهِبٌ إِلَىٰ رَبِّى سَيَهْدِينِ
- 37:100 رَبِّ هَبْ لِى مِنَ ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ
- 37:101 فَبَشَّرْنَٰهُ بِغُلَٰمٍ حَلِيمٍۢ
- 37:102 فَلَمَّا بَلَغَ مَعَهُ ٱلسَّعْىَ قَالَ يَٰبُنَىَّ إِنِّىٓ أَرَىٰ فِى ٱلْمَنَامِ أَنِّىٓ أَذْبَحُكَ فَٱنظُرْ مَاذَا تَرَىٰ ۚ قَالَ يَٰٓأَبَتِ ٱفْعَلْ مَا تُؤْمَرُ ۖ سَتَجِدُنِىٓ إِن شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مِنَ ٱلصَّٰبِرِينَ
- 37:103 فَلَمَّآ أَسْلَمَا وَتَلَّهُۥ لِلْجَبِينِ
- 37:104 وَنَٰدَيْنَٰهُ أَن يَٰٓإِبْرَٰهِيمُ
- 37:105 قَدْ صَدَّقْتَ ٱلرُّءْيَآ ۚ إِنَّا كَذَٰلِكَ نَجْزِى ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ
- 37:106 إِنَّ هَٰذَا لَهُوَ ٱلْبَلَٰٓؤُا۟ ٱلْمُبِينُ
- 37:107 وَفَدَيْنَٰهُ بِذِبْحٍ عَظِيمٍۢ
- 37:108 وَتَرَكْنَا عَلَيْهِ فِى ٱلْءَاخِرِينَ
- 37:109 سَلَٰمٌ عَلَىٰٓ إِبْرَٰهِيمَ
- 37:110 كَذَٰلِكَ نَجْزِى ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ
- 37:111 إِنَّهُۥ مِنْ عِبَادِنَا ٱلْمُؤْمِنِينَ
- 37:112 وَبَشَّرْنَٰهُ بِإِسْحَٰقَ نَبِيًّۭا مِّنَ ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ
- 37:113 وَبَٰرَكْنَا عَلَيْهِ وَعَلَىٰٓ إِسْحَٰقَ ۚ وَمِن ذُرِّيَّتِهِمَا مُحْسِنٌۭ وَظَالِمٌۭ لِّنَفْسِهِۦ مُبِينٌۭ
- 37:114 وَلَقَدْ مَنَنَّا عَلَىٰ مُوسَىٰ وَهَٰرُونَ
- 37:115 وَنَجَّيْنَٰهُمَا وَقَوْمَهُمَا مِنَ ٱلْكَرْبِ ٱلْعَظِيمِ
- 37:116 وَنَصَرْنَٰهُمْ فَكَانُوا۟ هُمُ ٱلْغَٰلِبِينَ
- 37:117 وَءَاتَيْنَٰهُمَا ٱلْكِتَٰبَ ٱلْمُسْتَبِينَ

===== _commentary/v16/work/s037/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ص ف ف (root_000871): 37:1 وَٱلصَّٰٓفَّٰتِ, 37:1 صَفًّا, 37:165 ٱلصَّآفُّونَ

- **B001** düz bir sıra oluşturma — düz çizgi üzerinde yan yana duran ögelerden oluşan sıra · yan yana sıraya dizmek · yan yana sıraya girmek · sıra halinde duran; kanatlarını açıp sabit tutan · savaş sırasının kurulduğu mevki · sıra halinde dizilmiş
  الصف معروف (ayn;tahdhib); الصف أن تجعل الشيء على خط مستو (mufradat); صففت القوم فاصطفوا (ayn;sihah;tahdhib); المصف الموقف والجمع المصاف (ayn;sihah;tahdhib); الصافات صفا يعني الملائكة (mufradat;tahdhib); الطير الصواف التي تصف أجنحتها فلا تحركها (ayn;tahdhib); البدن الصواف التي تصفف ثم تنحر (ayn;tahdhib)
- **B002** bir sağımda birden çok kabı dolduran ya da ön ayaklarını hizalayan dişi deve — bir sağımda birden çok kabı dolduran ya da ön ayaklarını hizalayan dişi deve · dişi deveyi tek sağımda iki veya üç kaba sağma
  الصفوف الناقة التي تجمع بين محلبين في حلبة (maqayis;tahdhib); الصف أن تحلب الناقة في محلبين أو ثلاثة تصف بينها (sihah); ناقة صفوف للتي تصف أقداحا من لبنها (sihah); الصفوف ناقة تصف بين محلبين فصاعدا لغزارتها (mufradat); الصفوف أيضا التي تصف يديها عند الحلب (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib)
- **B003** kurutmak ya da közlemek için sıra sıra serilmiş et — güneşte kurutulmak veya közde pişirilmek üzere sıra sıra serilmiş et · eti şeritlere ayırıp sıra sıra sermek · eti genişletip inceltecek biçimde dilimleme
  الصفيف قال قوم هو القديد (maqayis); اللحم يحمل في الأسفار طبيخا أو شواء فلا ينضج (maqayis); الصفيف القديد اذا شر في الشمس (ayn;tahdhib); الصفيف ما صف من اللحم على الجمر لينشوي (sihah); صففت اللحم قددته وألقيته صفا صفا (mufradat); التصفيف نحو التشريح (tahdhib)
- **B004** yapı ya da eyer bölümü — yapıda ya da eyerde bulunan bölüm veya düzenek · hayvan için eyer düzeneği yapmak
  الصفة من البنيان والسرج ايضا (ayn); صفة الدار والسرج واحدة الصفف (sihah); صفة السرج (tahdhib); صففت للدابة صفة أي عملتها له (tahdhib); الصفة من البنيان وصفة السرج (mufradat)
- **B005** düz ve pürüzsüz arazi — düz ve pürüzsüz, kimi kullanımda bitkisiz arazi · düz ve pürüzsüz araziler
  الصفصف وهو المستوي من الأرض (maqayis); الصفصف الفلاة المستوية الملساء (ayn); الصفصف المستوى من الأرض (sihah); الصفصف الذي لا نبات فيه (tahdhib); الصفصف القرعاء (tahdhib); الصفصف المستوي الأملس (tahdhib); الصفصف المستوي من الأرض كأنه على صف واحد (mufradat)
- **B006** söğüt ağacı — söğüt ağacı
  الصفصف شجر الخلاف الواحدة بالهاء (ayn); الصفصاف شجر الخلاف (sihah;mufradat); الصفصاف الخلاف (tahdhib); هو شجر الخلاف بلغة أهل الشام (tahdhib)
- **B007** su başında toplanma [kalıp] — su başında toplanmak · su başında toplanmak
  تضافوا على الماء وتصافوا عليه بمعنى واحد إذا اجتمعوا عليه (tahdhib)

## ز ج ر (root_000624): 37:2 فَٱلزَّٰجِرَٰتِ, 37:2 زَجْرًا, 37:19 زَجْرَةٌ

- **B001** sertçe engelleme; hayvanı sesle yürütme — sertçe engelleme ve azarlama · azarlayıp engelledi veya sesle uzaklaştırdı · engelledi ya da caydırdı · geri durdu veya vazgeçti · deveyi seslenerek sürdü · tek bir sert sesleniş · bulutları sesle süren melekler · kötü işlerden uzaklaştıran uyarı · gür ve uğultulu ses
  زجرته فانزجر أي نهيته (ayn)؛ زجرت الرجل أو السبع وهو انتهارك إياه (jamhara)؛ الزجر المنع والنهي؛ زجر البعير أي ساقه (sihah)؛ زجرت البعير حتى ثار ومضى؛ للبعير كالحث بلفظ؛ الزجر للإبل والدواب والسباع (tahdhib)؛ الزجر طرد بصوت؛ يستعمل في الطرد تارة وفي الصوت أخرى (mufradat)؛ كلمة تدل على الانتهار؛ زجرت البعير حتى مضى؛ زجرت فلانا عن الشيء فانزجر (maqayis)؛ الزمجرة الصوت والميم فيه زائدة وأصله من الزجر (maqayis)
- **B002** hayvan hareketini iyiye veya kötüye yorma — kuş ve benzeri hayvanların geçişini iyiye veya kötüye yorma · hayvanların görünüş ve geçişinden gelecek için anlam çıkarma · hayvan hareketlerini yorumlayıp geleceği kestiren kişi
  زجر الطير أن يقول الإنسان إذا رأى طائرا أو ظبيا أو نحوه (ayn)؛ زجر الطائر وهو التفاؤل به (jamhara)؛ الزجر العيافة وهو ضرب من التكهن (sihah)؛ الزجر للطير وغيرها التيمن بسنوحها أو التشاؤم ببروحها (tahdhib)
- **B003** iri bir balık türü — iri bir balık türü; kimi anlatımlarda küçük pullu · bu balık türünü bildiren çoğul ad
  الزجر ضرب من السمك عظام صغار الحرشف ويجمع الزجور (ayn)؛ الزجر ضرب من الحيتان عظام (jamhara)؛ الزجر ضرب من السمك عظام والجميع الزجور (tahdhib)
- **B004** görünüşten tanıyıp kokudan yadırgayan; sütünü esirgeyen deve — görünüşten tanıyıp kokudan yadırgayan deve · yavruya burnuyla yakınlık gösterip sütünü vermeyen dişi deve
  الزجور من الإبل التي تعرف بعينها وتنكر بأنفها (maqayis)؛ الزجور من الإبل التي تعرف بعينها وتنكر بأنفها (sihah)؛ الناقة العلوق زجور؛ التي ترأم بأنفها وتمنع درها (tahdhib)
- **B005** sırtı çökmüş veya yaralı; sağrısı ağır deve — omurları çökmüş ya da sırtı yaralı deve · sağrısı ağır olduğu için zor kalkan dişi deve
  الأزجر من الإبل الذي في فقار ظهره انخزال أو من دبر؛ ناقة زجراء وهي التي في وركيها ثقل فلا تكاد تقوم (ayn)

## ت ل و (root_000186): 37:3 فَٱلتَّٰلِيَٰتِ

- **B001** ardından izleme — bir şeyin ardından gelen · onu peşinden izledi · ay onun ardından sıra ve örnek alma bakımından geldi · atlar art arda geldi · hakkımı tam alıncaya kadar izini sürdüm
  أصل واحد وهو الاتباع (maqayis)؛ تلو الشيء الذي يتلوه (sihah)؛ تلاه تبعه متابعة (mufradat)؛ جاءت الخيل تتاليا أي متتابعة (sihah)

- **B004** bağlı güvence ve talep — kişiyi izleyen güvence veya yükümlülük · ona güvence verdim · birini hakkı nedeniyle başka birine yönelttim
  التلاء الذمة لأنها تتبع وتطلب يقال أتليته ذمة (maqayis)؛ التلاء الذمة (sihah)؛ أتليته أحلته من الحوالة (sihah)؛ أتليت فلانا على فلان بحق أي أحلته عليه (mufradat)

- **B008** son nefeste olmak [kalıp] — adam son nefesindeydi

  الجرامة ما سقط من التمر إذا جرم؛ الجرام والجريم التمر اليابس (maqayis)؛ الجرامة ما سقط من التمر إذا جرم؛ الجريم النوى وهما أيضا التمر اليابس (sihah)؛ الجرامة ما التقط من التمر بعدما يصرم؛ الجريم النوى وقيل البؤرة التي يرضخ فيها النوى؛ الجرام والجريم هما النوى وهما أيضا التمر اليابس؛ المد يدعى بالحجاز جريما (tahdhib)

  جرم أي كسب لأن الذي يحوزه فكأنه اقتطعه؛ فلان جريمة أهله أي كاسبهم؛ جرمت فزارة أي كسبتهم غضبا (maqayis)؛ جرم يجرم أي كسب؛ فلان جريمة أهله أي كاسبهم؛ ولا يجرمنكم أي لا يحملنكم ويقال لا يكسبنكم (sihah)؛ خرج يجرم قومه أي يكسبهم؛ لا يحملنكم ولا يكسبنكم؛ أجرمني كذا وجرمني وجرمت وأجرمت بمعنى واحد (tahdhib)

  فلان له جريمة أي جرم وهو مصدر الجارم الذي يجرم على نفسه وقومه شرا؛ الجرم الذنب وفعله الإجرام والمجرم المذنب والجارم الجاني (ayn)؛ الجرم الذنب والجريمة مثله؛ جرم وأجرم واجترم بمعنى (sihah)؛ الجرم مصدر الجارم؛ الجارم الجاني والمجرم والمذنب؛ لا يدخلنكم في الجرم؛ الجرم التعدي والجرم الذنب (tahdhib)؛ الجرم والجريمة الذنب وهو من الأول لأنه كسب (maqayis)

  لا جرم يجري مجرى لا بد ويفسر حقا (ayn)؛ لاجرم كانت في الأصل بمنزلة لا بد ولا محالة ثم صارت بمنزلة حقا؛ لا جرم لآتينك (sihah)؛ لا جرم بمنزلة لا بد ولا محالة؛ صارت بمنزلة حقا؛ لا ذا جرم ولا جر (tahdhib)؛ لا جرم هو من قولهم جرمت أي كسبت (maqayis)

  أقمت عنده حولا مجرما أي حولا تاما حتى انقضى؛ جرمنا هذه السنة أي خرجنا منها وتجرمت السنة والشتاء والصيف (ayn)؛ حول مجرم وسنة محرمة أي تامة؛ تجرمت السنون أي انقضت؛ تجرم الليل ذهب (sihah)؛ سنة مجرمة وشهر مجرم ويوم مجرم وهو التام؛ جرمنا هذه السنة أي خرجنا منها؛ تجرمت السنة؛ كله من الجرم وهو القطع (tahdhib)؛ سنة مجرمة أي تامة كأنها تصرمت عن تمام؛ تجرم الليل ذهب (maqayis)

## ن ذ ر (root_001488): 37:72 مُّنذِرِينَ, 37:73 ٱلْمُنذَرِينَ, 37:177 ٱلْمُنذَرِينَ

- **B001** tehlikeyi bildirerek sakındırma — uyarı amacıyla korkulacak bir şeyi bildirme · bir topluluğa korkulacak bir durumu haber verip sakındırmak · uyaran kişi veya uyarının kendisi · uyaranlar ya da uyarılar · birbirini korkutucu bir tehlikeye karşı uyarmak · düşmandan haberdar olup hazırlık ve sakınma durumuna geçmek · ani tehlikeyi haber veren kişi için kullanılan temsil · önceden ceza veya sonuç bildiren kişinin gerekçesini tamamladığını anlatan söz · ordunun düşman durumunu bildiren öncü gözcüsü

  النذر وهو أنه يخاف إذا أخلف؛ النذر أيضا ما يجب كأنه نذر أي أوجب (maqayis)؛ النذر واحد النذور؛ نذرت لله كذا؛ نذر على نفسه نذرا (sihah)؛ النذر ما ينذره الإنسان فيجعله على نفسه نحبا واجبا؛ نذرت على نفسي أي أوجبت؛ النذر ما كان وعدا على شرط (tahdhib)؛ النذر أن توجب على نفسك ما ليس بواجب لحدوث أمر؛ نذرت لله أمرا (mufradat)

  السعاية في أخذ الصدقات (maqayis)؛ الساعي الذي يولى قبض الصدقات والجمع سعاة (ayn)؛ من ولى شيئا على قوم فهو ساع عليهم وأكثر ما يقال ذلك في ولاة الصدقة (sihah)؛ الساعي الذي يقوم بأمر أصحابه عند السلطان؛ عامل الصدقات ساع؛ ساعي اليهود والنصارى هو رئيسهم؛ من ولى عملا على قوم فهو ساع عليهم (tahdhib)؛ خصت السعاية بأخذ الصدقة (mufradat)
- **B004** birini üst makama kötüleyerek ihbar etme — üst makama kötüleyerek ihbar etme · birini yöneticiye kötüleyerek ihbar etmek · üst makama söz taşıyan ihbarcı
  السعاية أن تسعى بصاحبك إلى وال أو من فوقه (ayn)؛ سعى به إلى الوالي إذا وشى به (sihah)؛ الساعي الذي يسعى بصاحبه إلى سلطانه؛ القتات والساعي والماحل واحد؛ الساعي مثلث بإهلاكه ثلاثة نفر (tahdhib)؛ خصت السعاية بالنميمة (mufradat)
- **B005** özgürlük bedelini çalışarak ödeme — köleleştirilmiş kişinin özgürlüğü için çalışması · özgürlük bedelini çalışarak kazanma · özgürlük sözleşmesinin bedelini çalışarak ödemek · köleleştirilmiş kişiyi kendi bedeli için çalıştırmak · kalan özgürlük bedelini çalışarak ödeyen kişi
  سعاية العبد إذا كوتب أن يسعى فيما يفك رقبته (maqayis)؛ السعاية ما يستسعى فيه العبد من ثمن رقبته (ayn)؛ سعى المكاتب في عتق رقبته سعاية؛ استسعيت العبد في قيمته (sihah)؛ استسعاء العبد إذا عتق بعضه ورق بعضه؛ يستسعى في ثلثي رقبته (tahdhib)؛ خصت السعاية بكسب المكاتب لعتق رقبته (mufradat)
- **B006** övünç getiren soylu ve cömert iş — cömertlikle kazanılan onurlu iş ve başarı · övünç veren onurlu işler ve başarılar · barış için bedel üstlenen uzlaştırıcılar
  المسعاة في الكرم والجود (maqayis)؛ المسعاة في الكرم والجود (ayn)؛ المسعاة واحدة المساعي في الكرم والجود (sihah)؛ أصحاب الحمالات لحقن الدماء وإطفاء النائرة سعاة؛ مآثر أهل الشرف والفضل مساعي واحدتها مسعاة (tahdhib)؛ المسعاة بطلب المكرمة (mufradat)
- **B007** köleleştirilmiş kadınla ilişki veya onu cinsel kazanca zorlama — köleleştirilmiş bir kadınla evlilik dışı cinsel ilişkiye girmek · köleleştirilmiş kadınlarla sınırlı evlilik dışı ilişki veya onları cinsel kazanca zorlama
  ساعي الرجل الأمة إذا فجر بها؛ لا تكون المساعاة إلا في الإماء خاصة (maqayis)؛ يقال في الأمة خاصة قد ساعاها؛ لا تكون المساعاة إلا في الإماء؛ إماء ساعين في الجاهلية (sihah)؛ المساعاة الزنى؛ لا تكون في الحرائر إنما تكون في الإماء؛ مساعاة الأمة إذ ساعاها مالكها فضرب عليها ضريبة تؤديها بالزنى (tahdhib)؛ خصت المساعاة بالفجور (mufradat)
- **B008** aynı uğraşta rakibini yenme — benimle aynı uğraşta yarıştı, ben de onu yendim

  تبارك الله أي بارك (sihah)؛ تبارك ارتفع؛ تبارك تعالى وتعاظم؛ تبارك الله تمجيد وتعظيم؛ تبارك تقدس (tahdhib)؛ كل موضع ذكر فيه لفظ تبارك فهو تنبيه على اختصاصه تعالى بالخيرات المذكورة (mufradat)؛ تبارك الله تمجيد وتجليل؛ وفسر على تعالى الله (maqayis)

(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s037/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s037 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 - Regulated Order and Access
- Semantic invariant: A ranked order is maintained by controlled speech, guarded boundaries, and the interception of unauthorized approach.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:1-10 presents ranks, recitation, a guarded sky, attempted hearing, and pursuit, with the summons resumed at 37:19.
- Surprising reach: Military formation, choral succession, architectural enclosure, and projectile interception converge on one access-control process.

#### Subchannel A. P1 - Vocal Formation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A straight rank issues a driving command through successive voices until multiplicity is directed as one.
- Active motifs: straight formation `quranic:root_000871:B001/m01`; vocal drive or rebuke `quranic:root_000624:B001/m01`; succession `quranic:root_000186:B001/m01`; voice following voice `quranic:root_000186:B007/m01`; articulation on the tongue `quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`; unification in one direction `quranic:root_001631:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:1 (ص ف ف); 37:2 (ز ج ر); 37:3 (ت ل و, ذ ك ر); 37:4, 37:19 (و ح د)
- Synthesis: The opening oaths activate not just ordered beings but an acoustic chain of command whose force lies in coordinated succession.

#### Subchannel B. P1 - Ornamented Enclosure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A roof-like upper boundary is beautified, encircled, and maintained as a protected perimeter.
- Active motifs: sky as roof or cloud-cover `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; adornment `quranic:root_000660:B002/m01`; star `quranic:root_001331:B001/m01`; guarding care `quranic:root_000342:B001/m01`; encircling crown or cloud `quranic:root_001315:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:5-6 (س م و); 37:6 (ز ي ن, ك و ك ب); 37:7 (ح ف ظ, ك ل ل); 37:8 (ك ل ل)
- Synthesis: Celestial beauty materializes as perimeter architecture: ornament and defense are two functions of the same enclosure.

#### Subchannel C. P1 - Intercepted Audition
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A prohibited listener is detected, struck, forced into pursuit, and exposed by a piercing flare.
- Active motifs: hearing sounds `quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`; projectile casting `quranic:root_001209:B001/m01`; swift snatching `quranic:root_000423:B001/m01`; pursuit and overtaking `quranic:root_000175:B002/m01`; meteor-like flame `quranic:root_000821:B002/m01`; piercing light or ignition `quranic:root_000200:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:8 (س م ع, ق ذ ف); 37:10 (خ ط ف, ت ب ع, ش ه ب, ث ق ب)
- Synthesis: The scene is an interception protocol rather than a static barrier: illicit reception triggers capture, chase, and visible exposure.

### 2. P1 - Decisive Form
- Semantic invariant: An initially indeterminate body or case is measured, fixed, examined, and brought to a separating decision.
- Surface relation: direct; adhesive clay is the created material at 37:11, while sight, reckoning, and the Day of Separation close the interval at 37:19-21.
- Surprising reach: Pottery, plastering, sealing, visual inspection, and legal verdict share the operation of making an unstable matter determinate.

#### Subchannel A. P1 - Measured and Sealed Clay
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Material is estimated before cutting, smoothed, mixed, made adhesive, and capable of receiving a seal.
- Active motifs: measure before cutting `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; creation `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; smoothing a surface `quranic:root_000434:B008/m01`; mixed clay `quranic:root_000963:B001/m01`; clay coating or plaster `quranic:root_000963:B002/m01`; clay document-seal `quranic:root_000963:B003/m01`; adhesion and interpenetration `quranic:root_001353:B002/m01`; binding tightly `quranic:root_000782:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:11 (خ ل ق, ط ي ن, ل ز ب, ش د د)
- Synthesis: The clay image pressures the primary creation reading toward manufacture: proportioning, adhesion, surface finishing, and authentication are latent in the material.

#### Subchannel B. P1 - Inquiry and Separating Verdict
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Looking and clarification culminate in a judgment that marks a boundary between opposed claims.
- Active motifs: requesting a ruling `quranic:root_001130:B004/m01`; visual or intellectual inspection `quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`; reckoning or judgment `quranic:root_000504:B002/m01`; separation or boundary-marking `quranic:root_001159:B001/m01`; verdict separating truth and falsehood `quranic:root_001159:B002/m01`; accusation of falsehood `quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`; grave event or day `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:11 (ف ت ي); 37:19 (ن ظ ر); 37:20 (د ي ن, ي و م); 37:21 (ف ص ل, ك ذ ب, ي و م)
- Synthesis: Resurrection is reframed as adjudication: the same instant that makes bodies present also makes disputed truth finally separable.

### 3. P2 - Captive Tribunal
- Semantic invariant: Agents are sorted, driven, detained, confronted, and assigned liability through reciprocal testimony.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:22-39 moves from gathering and guidance to a halt, cross-questioning, shared punishment, and recompense.
- Surprising reach: Herding, captivity, face-to-face litigation, oath, proof, and accounting form one coercive judicial workflow.

#### Subchannel A. P2 - Sorted Conveyance
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A mixed population is classified by affiliation, gathered, subordinated, and driven along a designated road.
- Active motifs: gathering and driving to a destination `quranic:root_000324:B001/m01`; peers grouped by a shared act `quranic:root_000652:B005/m01`; subjugation or enslavement `quranic:root_000973:B004/m01`; a leader or vanguard going first `quranic:root_001583:B003/m01`; road `quranic:root_000858:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:22 (ح ش ر, ز و ج, ع ب د); 37:23 (ه د ي, ص ر ط)
- Synthesis: “Gather” and “guide” become an involuntary classification-and-transfer operation, not beneficent guidance.

#### Subchannel B. P2 - Halted Face-Off
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The convoy is confined, opposing parties face one another, and former dependents are handed over as captives.
- Active motifs: halt or confinement `quranic:root_001676:B001/m01`; opposing parties facing off `quranic:root_001676:B006/m01`; handover or relinquishment `quranic:root_000737:B012/m01`; taking captive `quranic:root_000737:B013/m01`; face-to-face orientation `quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:24 (و ق ف); 37:26 (س ل م); 37:27 (ق ب ل)
- Synthesis: The halted crowd is staged as a captive confrontation in which hierarchy no longer shields either side.

#### Subchannel C. P2 - Claims Under Compulsion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Reciprocal questioning tests whether oath, force, or proof can establish a valid right.
- Active motifs: mutual questioning `quranic:root_000661:B004/m01`; oath `quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`; power or right `quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`; coercive authority `quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`; compelling proof `quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`; litigants each claiming a right `quranic:root_000347:B004/m01`; establishing or displaying truth `quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:24, 37:27 (س ء ل); 37:28 (ي م ن); 37:30 (س ل ط); 37:31, 37:37 (ح ق ق)
- Synthesis: The exchange exposes power as an invalid defense: neither coercion nor assertion substitutes for a demonstrated right.

#### Subchannel D. P2 - Co-Produced Liability
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Participation in deviation becomes a jointly experienced penalty calibrated to acquired acts.
- Active motifs: undergoing or tasting punishment `quranic:root_000526:B002/m01`; cooperation in evil `quranic:root_001116:B005/m01`; shared participation `quranic:root_000791:B001/m01`; earning or acquisition `quranic:root_000239:B003/m01`; offense or crime `quranic:root_000239:B004/m01`; recompense `quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`; intentional work `quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:31, 37:38 (ذ و ق); 37:32 (غ و ي); 37:33 (ش ر ك); 37:34 (ج ر م); 37:39 (ج ز ي, ع م ل)
- Synthesis: Shared punishment is not guilt by proximity; it materializes the shared production of the misleading practice.

### 4. P3 - Intact Provision
- Semantic invariant: A protected allotment is served in ordered circulation and remains pleasurable without depletion or hidden injury.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:40-49 describes assigned provision, honor, facing couches, a circulating cup, pure drink, and guarded companions.
- Surprising reach: Rationing, hospitality service, vessel circulation, toxicology, exhaustion, and protected nesting jointly define incorrupt provision.

#### Subchannel A. P3 - Allotted Service
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An exclusive share of food is presented with honor to seated recipients while service circulates among them.
- Active motifs: exclusive or assigned share `quranic:root_000430:B004/m01`; allotted provision `quranic:root_000560:B001/m01`; food `quranic:root_000560:B002/m01`; fruit `quranic:root_001174:B002/m01`; honor or generosity `quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`; good living `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`; couch or seat `quranic:root_000697:B011/m01`; face-to-face orientation `quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`; circulating around `quranic:root_000957:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:40 (خ ل ص); 37:41 (ر ز ق); 37:42 (ف ك ه, ك ر م); 37:43 (ن ع م); 37:44 (س ر ر, ق ب ل); 37:45 (ط و ف)
- Synthesis: Reward appears as a reliably administered allotment whose abundance is expressed through dignified service rather than accumulation.

#### Subchannel B. P3 - Pure, Unexhausted Draught
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A spring-fed, bright drink preserves flavor while excluding admixture, seizure, intoxication, and depletion.
- Active motifs: purification after admixture `quranic:root_000430:B001/m01`; spring `quranic:root_001069:B006/m01`; whiteness `quranic:root_000168:B001/m01`; pleasant taste `quranic:root_001352:B001/m01`; drinking `quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`; hidden harmful seizure `quranic:root_001115:B001/m01`; mind drained by intoxication `quranic:root_001491:B003/m01`; drink exhausted `quranic:root_001491:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:40 (خ ل ص); 37:45 (ع ي ن); 37:46 (ب ي ض, ل ذ ذ, ش ر ب); 37:47 (غ و ل, ن ز ف)
- Synthesis: Purity is operational: the draught does not conceal damage, remove judgment, or run out.

### 5. P4 - Punitive Catering
- Semantic invariant: Hospitality is inverted into a meal whose source, appearance, ingestion, admixture, and destination all intensify harm.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:62-68 introduces hostile “lodging,” the tree, forced filling, a hot mixture, and return to the furnace.
- Surprising reach: Guest-fare, botany, snake anatomy, feeding vessels, adulteration, reheating, and furnace operation expose punishment as a complete catering process.

#### Subchannel A. P4 - Furnace-Rooted Fare
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An offered course grows from the furnace floor and is altered by the heat that sustains it.
- Active motifs: guest-fare or lodging `quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`; tree `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`; forced swallowing `quranic:root_000636:B002/m01`; assay or test `quranic:root_001128:B001/m01`; burning or blackening `quranic:root_001128:B002/m01`; root or base `quranic:root_000038:B001/m01`; furnace `quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:62 (ن ز ل, ش ج ر, ز ق م); 37:63 (ف ت ن); 37:64 (ء ص ل, ش ج ر, ج ح م)
- Synthesis: The hostile fare is botanically continuous with its punitive setting: furnace heat tests, alters, and feeds what grows from its base.

#### Subchannel B. P4 - Repellent Yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The furnace-rooted growth produces shoots whose heads are perceived through snake-like morphology.
- Active motifs: snake `quranic:root_000038:B004/m01`; fruit or shoot `quranic:root_000945:B005/m01`; head or top `quranic:root_000529:B001/m01`; ugly snake-like thing `quranic:root_000796:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:64 (ء ص ل); 37:65 (ط ل ع, ر ء س, ش ط ن)

### 7. P5 - Integrity, Diagnosis, and Withdrawal

### 8. P5 - Craft Reversal

### 11. P6 - Competing Proprietorship and Irrigation

- Active motifs: swallowing `quranic:root_001371:B002/m01`; plugging the mouth or silencing `quranic:root_001371:B003/m01`; fish `quranic:root_000366:B001/m01`; maneuvering `quranic:root_000366:B002/m01`; blame `quranic:root_001386:B001/m01`; swimming `quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`; confinement or remaining `quranic:root_001339:B001/m01`; interior or belly `quranic:root_000128:B001/m01`

- Synthesis: Removal from the ship leads to a second, bodily vessel in which capture includes both immobilization and loss of voice.

#### Subchannel C. P7 - Exposed Convalescence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The confined figure is cast onto open ground in feverish exposure and covered through plant-like, child-like nurture.
- Active motifs: casting away `quranic:root_001466:B001/m01`; abandoned child `quranic:root_001466:B007/m01`; stripped or exposed `quranic:root_001005:B003/m01`; open ground `quranic:root_001005:B004/m01`; chills or fever `quranic:root_001005:B007/m01`; illness `quranic:root_000721:B001/m01`; planting or cultivation `quranic:root_001465:B002/m01`; rearing or nurturing a child `quranic:root_001465:B006/m01`; tree `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:145 (ن ب ذ, ع ر ي, س ق م); 37:146 (ن ب ت, ش ج ر)
- Synthesis: Expulsion reverses confinement but creates vulnerability; cultivation supplies the cover and care needed to restore bodily viability.

#### Subchannel D. P7 - Restored Dispatch
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An initial commission interrupted by flight is restored as dispatch to an assembled population that increases, believes, and is preserved until a set time.
- Active motifs: dispatching `quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`; assembling or connecting `quranic:root_000045:B002/m01`; increase `quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`; belief `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`; preserving for temporary use `quranic:root_001395:B007/m01`; set time `quranic:root_000382:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:139, 37:147 (ر س ل); 37:147 (ء ل ف, ز ي د); 37:148 (ء م ن, م ت ع, ح ي ن)

### 15. P7 - Genealogical Claim Trial
- Semantic invariant: A reproductive allocation claim is forced into evidentiary procedure: its witnesses, selection rule, judgment, authority, and document must be produced.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:149-157 interrogates daughters, sons, creation, witnessing, offspring, selection, judgment, proof, and a purported book.
- Surprising reach: Marriage contract, childbirth tissue, royal allotment, delegated arbitration, deed, and binding decree pressure theology as a defective genealogy case.

#### Subchannel A. P7 - Claimed Reproductive Event
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A requested ruling tests the alleged female creation against contract, sex, witnessed birth, and its physical evidence.
- Active motifs: requesting a ruling `quranic:root_001130:B004/m01`; sons, daughters, or lineage `quranic:root_000156:B007/m01`; creation `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; marriage contract `quranic:root_001444:B004/m01`; female `quranic:root_000058:B001/m01`; witnessing `quranic:root_000822:B001/m01`; afterbirth `quranic:root_000822:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:149 (ف ت ي, ب ن ي); 37:150 (خ ل ق, م ل ك, ء ن ث, ش ه د)
- Synthesis: The genealogy lacks an observable reproductive event: neither creative participation, contract, witness, nor birth evidence supports it.

#### Subchannel B. P7 - Attributed Offspring and Reserved Share
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An unsaid claim assigns offspring through childbirth language and alleges that the chooser reserved a preferred share.
- Active motifs: lie `quranic:root_000041:B002/m01`; attributing what was not said `quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`; offspring `quranic:root_001683:B001/m01`; childbirth `quranic:root_001683:B003/m01`; selection `quranic:root_000873:B002/m01`; ruler’s chosen share `quranic:root_000873:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:151 (ء ف ك, ق و ل); 37:152 (و ل د); 37:153 (ص ف و)
- Synthesis: The false genealogy is also a false allocation: invented birth is used to justify an invented preference and reserved portion.

#### Subchannel C. P7 - Verdict, Proof, and Document
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The claimant is asked to supply a valid judgment, recollection of right, compelling authority, and the text allegedly guaranteeing the claim.
- Active motifs: judgment `quranic:root_000348:B002/m01`; delegated arbitration `quranic:root_000348:B005/m01`; recollection `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; deed or document of right `quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`; compelling proof `quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`; evident clarity `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`; producing or bringing `quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`; written document `quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`; binding decree `quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`; truth `quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:154 (ح ك م); 37:155 (ذ ك ر); 37:156 (س ل ط, ب ي ن); 37:157 (ء ت ي, ك ت ب, ص د ق)
- Synthesis: The passage converts assertion into a documentary burden of proof and finds no valid ruling, authority, or instrument.

### 16. P8 - Description Governs Relation and Destination
- Semantic invariant: Description assigns relation and destination: invented description creates a trapping connection, while exonerating speech removes that description from its true referent.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:158-163 rejects fabricated kinship and follows seduction to the fire, while 37:180 repeats the rejection of false description through exaltation.
- Surprising reach: Genealogical proportion, brokerage, sale by description, hunting trap, and juridical exoneration expose theology as consequential attribution.

#### Subchannel A. P8 - Fabricated Kinship
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A relation is appointed between visible and hidden parties through claimed lineage and proportional connection.
- Active motifs: causative assignment to a state or status `quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`; connecting bond `quranic:root_000170:B003/m01`; hidden beings `quranic:root_000266:B005/m01`; lineage `quranic:root_001494:B001/m01`; proportional relation `quranic:root_001494:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:158 (ج ع ل, ب ي ن, ج ن ن, ن س ب)
- Synthesis: The error is relational before it is descriptive: an invented genealogy assigns a measured connection where none has been established.

#### Subchannel B. P8 - Brokered Unseen Description
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A go-between describes unseen parties, but their knowledge and compelled appearance expose the description’s failure.
- Active motifs: go-between or gossip `quranic:root_001494:B005/m01`; knowing `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; compelled legal appearance `quranic:root_000333:B006/m01`; describing `quranic:root_001654:B001/m01`; sale by unseen description `quranic:root_001654:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:158 (ن س ب, ع ل م, ح ض ر); 37:159 (و ص ف)
- Synthesis: The kinship claim behaves like a brokered transaction in an unseen object, invalidated when the absent party’s knowledge and liability enter the forum.

#### Subchannel C. P8 - Selective Seduction as Trap
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Purified service remains outside the diversion, while those who enter its trapping relation meet the fire.
- Active motifs: purification after admixture `quranic:root_000430:B001/m01`; worship or obedient service `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`; seduction or diversion `quranic:root_001128:B003/m01`; meeting fire `quranic:root_000880:B003/m01`; hunting trap `quranic:root_000880:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:160 (ع ب د, خ ل ص); 37:161 (ع ب د); 37:162 (ف ت ن); 37:163 (ص ل ي)
- Synthesis: Seduction is selective rather than universal: purified allegiance excludes capture, while the trapping route already contains its terminal encounter.

#### Subchannel D. P8 - Exoneration From False Description
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Repeated descriptions are cleared from the true referent by speech that asserts lordship and unassailable strength.
- Active motifs: exoneration or clearing from fault `quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`; describing `quranic:root_001654:B001/m01`; lordship or mastery `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; strength and honor after abasement `quranic:root_001008:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:159, 37:180 (س ب ح, و ص ف); 37:180 (ر ب ب, ع ز ز)
- Synthesis: Exaltation is corrective attribution: it removes fabricated descriptions and identifies the referent through mastery and invulnerable strength.

### 17. P8 - Claimed Warrant and Revealed Disposition
- Semantic invariant: A stated condition for obedience is supplied, and the response to its arrival reveals whether the earlier claim described a real disposition.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:167-170 records the claim that an ancestral reminder would produce purified service, followed by rejection of the reminder and promised knowledge.
- Surprising reach: Doctrinal statement, present possession, title-deed, precedent, filtration, concealment, and disclosure form a compact test of counterfactual sincerity.

#### Subchannel A. P8 - Claimed Ancestral Warrant
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speakers condition purified service on possessing a reminder backed by ancestral precedent and documentary right.
- Active motifs: spoken claim `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; doctrinal position `quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`; presence or possession `quranic:root_001052:B004/m01`; recollection `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; deed or document of right `quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`; temporal precedence `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`; purification after admixture `quranic:root_000430:B001/m01`; worship or obedient service `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:167 (ق و ل); 37:168 (ع ن د, ذ ك ر, ء و ل); 37:169 (خ ل ص, ع ب د)
- Synthesis: The claim treats obedience as awaiting a missing instrument, combining precedent, possession, and documentary authorization into an asserted condition.

#### Subchannel B. P8 - Covered Warrant Becomes Disclosure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: When the requested matter arrives, it is covered and denied, converting the promised future into eventual disclosure.
- Active motifs: covering or concealment `quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`; hiding or denying truth `quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`; knowledge through disclosure `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:170 (ك ف ر, ع ل م)
- Synthesis: Arrival removes the counterfactual excuse: concealment of the supplied warrant becomes the very evidence that will be made known.

### 18. P8 - Assigned Order Becomes Outcome
- Semantic invariant: A fixed station and disciplined rank mature into a promised campaign whose apparent delay ends in witnessed victory, dawn descent, and a final allocation of recognition.
- Surface relation: direct; 37:164-166 and 37:171-182 move from known stations and ranks through promised victory, waiting, accelerated punishment, morning arrival, peace, and praise.
- Surprising reach: Positional assignment, race order, military dispatch, shield-work, scouting, shortcut, courtyard occupation, dawn raid, and ceremonial recognition turn liturgical order into operational history.

#### Subchannel A. P8 - Station, Rank, and Race Order
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Each agent has a marked standing place, forms part of a straight rank, and moves in ordered succession.
- Active motifs: station or place `quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`; standing support or component `quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`; mark or standard `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; straight formation `quranic:root_000871:B001/m01`; swift swimming motion `quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`; runner following the winner `quranic:root_000880:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:163 (ص ل ي); 37:164 (ق و م, ع ل م); 37:165 (ص ف ف); 37:166 (س ب ح)
- Synthesis: Known station and rank define constrained agency: movement is active and swift, but it remains ordered relative to a prior place.

#### Subchannel B. P8 - Pledged Dispatch and Victory
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A word advances ahead of events as a pledge that dispatched servants will receive support and the army will conquer.
- Active motifs: advancing or racing `quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`; wager or prize `quranic:root_000671:B002/m01`; declared word `quranic:root_001316:B002/m01`; dispatching `quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`; support or victory `quranic:root_001510:B001/m01`; army `quranic:root_000264:B001/m01`; conquest `quranic:root_001098:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:171 (س ب ق, ك ل م, ر س ل); 37:172 (ن ص ر); 37:173 (ج ن د, غ ل ب)
- Synthesis: Promise functions as an advance commitment: the declared outcome precedes deployment and fixes what dispatched forces will attain.

#### Subchannel C. P8 - Watch, Acceleration, and Dawn Occupation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Observers turn aside and wait until reconnaissance, accelerated approach, and dawn arrival make the pledged defeat visible in the courtyard.
- Active motifs: turning aside `quranic:root_001684:B007/m01`; waiting for an interval `quranic:root_000382:B005/m01`; observing `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; shield or armor `quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`; haste `quranic:root_000987:B001/m01`; shortcut `quranic:root_000987:B008/m01`; punishment `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`; descending for battle `quranic:root_001492:B007/m01`; courtyard `quranic:root_000756:B001/m01`; defeat or misfortune `quranic:root_000755:B002/m01`; dawn raid `quranic:root_000839:B004/m01`; scout warning `quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`

- Synthesis: Delay is tactical rather than doubtful: waiting opens into reconnaissance, a shortened approach, and occupation at dawn.

#### Subchannel D. P8 - Allocated Peace and Praise
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The completed order distributes recognition by role: peace to message-bearers and praise to the sovereign Lord.
- Active motifs: peace against warfare `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`; messenger or carried message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; praise opposed to blame `quranic:root_000355:B001/m01`; lordship or mastery `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:181 (س ل م, ر س ل); 37:182 (ح م د, ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: The final formula is an ordered settlement of honor: carriers receive peace, while ultimate praise remains with the authority from whom the mission proceeds.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 - Sun-Dried Travel Meat
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Meat is spread in ordered strips, exposed to heat, dried for preservation, and made portable for travel.
- Active motifs: meat spread or dried for travel `quranic:root_000871:B003/m01`; sun-drying and preserving meat `quranic:root_000790:B002/m01`; burning flare `quranic:root_000821:B002/m01`; piercing ignition `quranic:root_000200:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:1 (ص ف ف); 37:5 (ش ر ق); 37:10 (ش ه ب, ث ق ب)
- Synthesis: The rank-and-flare vocabulary unexpectedly materializes a preservation scene in which ordered exposure to heat converts flesh into durable provision.

### S2. P3 - Companion, Lookout, and Rescue
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A remembered companion is inspected from a lookout position, found at the destructive midpoint, and recognized as a danger narrowly escaped.
- Active motifs: companion `quranic:root_001221:B002/m01`; inspection of a hidden matter `quranic:root_000945:B003/m01`; scout or lookout `quranic:root_000945:B004/m01`; sight `quranic:root_000531:B001/m01`; midpoint `quranic:root_000766:B006/m01`; fall into destruction `quranic:root_000558:B003/m01`; arduous attempt `quranic:root_001329:B001/m01`; nearly `quranic:root_001329:B002/m01`; escape from a snare `quranic:root_000430:B002/m01`; rescue or victory `quranic:root_001186:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:40 (خ ل ص); 37:51 (ق ر ن); 37:54 (ط ل ع); 37:55 (ط ل ع, ر ء ي, س و ي); 37:56 (ر د ي, ك و د); 37:60 (ف و ز)
- Synthesis: The exchange is organized as retrospective reconnaissance: the survivor sees both the former companion and the precise danger from which favor extracted him.

### S3. P3 - Sheltered Nest Image
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Restrained gazes and bright eyes are figured as eggs kept intact beneath a protective cover.
- Active motifs: restraining or confining the gaze `quranic:root_001231:B004/m01`; eye or gaze `quranic:root_000931:B002/m01`; broad beautiful eyes `quranic:root_001069:B016/m01`; bird egg `quranic:root_000168:B003/m01`; protecting cover `quranic:root_001324:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:48 (ق ص ر, ط ر ف, ع ي ن); 37:49 (ب ي ض, ك ن ن)
- Synthesis: The comparison activates a nest-like economy of intactness: beauty is coupled to restraint, enclosure, and protection from contact.

### S4. P4 - Inherited Tracks and Driven Outcome
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Those who stray follow surviving hoof-marks at speed until the trace becomes the means by which their end can be inspected.
- Active motifs: stray animal `quranic:root_000913:B005/m01`; surviving trace `quranic:root_000011:B003/m01`; following tracks `quranic:root_000011:B004/m01`; camel hoof-mark `quranic:root_000011:B008/m01`; violent driving or haste `quranic:root_001586:B001/m01`; wind carrying dust `quranic:root_001586:B007/m01`; scout warning `quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`; heel or trace `quranic:root_001033:B002/m01`; outcome `quranic:root_001033:B006/m01`; inspection `quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:69, 37:71 (ض ل ل); 37:70 (ء ث ر, ه ر ع); 37:72-73 (ن ذ ر); 37:73 (ع ق ب, ن ظ ر)
- Synthesis: Inheritance becomes track-following: ancestral precedent is not passive memory but a visible route along which followers are actively driven toward an inspectable outcome.

### S5. Aging Drink in a Stretched Skin
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hide is stretched into a storage vessel, drink is left there until it matures and strengthens, and a small portable skin is checked for a thin leaking point.
- Active motifs: vessel for aging drink over several days `quranic:root_000067:B010/m01`; stretching a hide vessel until it expands `quranic:root_001394:B003/m01`; drink becoming strong `quranic:root_001103:B003/m01`; small portable waterskin `quranic:root_000987:B004/m01`; leaking weak point in a hide vessel `quranic:root_001069:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:17, 37:59, 37:71, 37:126, 37:168 (ء و ل); 37:45, 37:48 (ع ي ن); 37:101 (غ ل م); 37:147 (م ء ي); 37:176 (ع ج ل)
- Synthesis: The storage operation materializes the surah's beverage polarity: ordinary drink gains quality and dangerous strength by waiting inside vulnerable hide, whereas the garden draught is immediately pure and the punitive draught is corrupting in itself.

### S6. Breeding and Morning-Milking a She-Camel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A she-camel is bred, her pregnancy and approaching birth become visible, pre-birth milk appears, and the overnight yield is drawn while she kneels in the morning and judged by whether it remains abundant.
- Active motifs: stallion covering a she-camel `quranic:root_000906:B011/m01`; pregnancy visible in body and udder `quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`; birth drawing near `quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`; pleasant milk appearing before birth `quranic:root_001174:B004/m01`; overnight milk drawn from a kneeling camel in the morning `quranic:root_000109:B007/m01`; abundant camel milk `quranic:root_000200:B005/m01`; milk that "lies" by failing to continue `quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:6 (د ن و); 37:10 (ث ق ب); 37:14, 37:55, 37:102, 37:105 (ر ء ي); 37:21, 37:127, 37:152 (ك ذ ب); 37:42 (ف ك ه); 37:93 (ض ر ب); 37:113 (ب ر ك)
- Synthesis: The husbandry sequence turns promised offspring and blessing into an observable test of generation and durable provision: pregnancy must appear, birth approach, and yield persist. Milk that "lies" by failing to last usefully pressures fabricated lineage and unsupported promise.

### S7. Fitting a Camel Saddle

- Ayah anchors: 37:16, 37:53, 37:60, 37:76, 37:107, 37:115 (ع ظ م); 37:37, 37:72, 37:123, 37:133, 37:139, 37:147, 37:171, 37:181 (ر س ل); 37:39, 37:61, 37:96 (ع م ل); 37:55 (س و ي); 37:63, 37:162 (ف ت ن); 37:66, 37:144 (ب ط ن); 37:90, 37:174, 37:178 (و ل ي)

### S8. Handling a Racehorse and the Forbidden Side Horse
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A racehorse is roped, brought to the run, given rein to accelerate, and assessed by its matched stride, while the wager excludes an auxiliary horse introduced beside the racer.
- Active motifs: rope placed through a horse's neck `quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`; rider extending the reins to increase speed `quranic:root_000151:B007/m01`; bringing a horse to the run `quranic:root_000333:B004/m01`; hard acceleration `quranic:root_001291:B008/m01`; matched fore-and-hind hoof placement `quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`; forbidden side horse in a wager `quranic:root_000262:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:8 (ج ن ب); 37:31, 37:37 (ح ق ق); 37:46 (ش ر ب); 37:57, 37:127, 37:158 (ح ض ر); 37:76, 37:115 (ك ر ب); 37:102 (ب ل غ)
- Synthesis: The race makes precedence auditable: speed and matched stride can establish a winner, while a side horse can falsify the wager. It therefore pressures boasts about who will prevail by contrasting regulated victory with manufactured advantage.

### S9. Testing a Bow and Reading a Miss
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An archer tests the bow's tension, draws it fully, takes an arrow from its quiver, and diagnoses whether the shot rises over, passes behind, or veers harmlessly from the target.
- Active motifs: testing a bow by drawing its string `quranic:root_000526:B003/m01`; drawing a bow to its limit `quranic:root_001080:B004/m01`; projectile arrow `quranic:root_000754:B001/m01`; arrow quiver `quranic:root_001324:B004/m01`; arrow rising over the target `quranic:root_000945:B010/m01`; arrow passing behind the target `quranic:root_000458:B018/m01`; shot veering away without a scratch `quranic:root_001464:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:31, 37:38 (ذ و ق); 37:49 (ك ن ن); 37:54, 37:55, 37:65 (ط ل ع); 37:82 (غ ر ق); 37:90 (د ب ر); 37:112 (ن ب ء); 37:141 (س ه م)
- Synthesis: The sequence distinguishes force from accuracy: even a tested bow at maximum draw can send an arrow over, behind, or away from the mark. That reframes the surah's tests, signs, and decisive outcomes around successful contact rather than display of power alone.

### S10. Raising Water by Rope and Pole
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A tightly twisted rope, one-handled bucket, balancing lug, and long lifting pole are assembled so water can be dipped and raised.
- Active motifs: long tightly twisted bucket rope `quranic:root_000796:B002/m01`; strengthening a rope by tight twisting `quranic:root_001414:B005/m01`; elongated one-handled water-carrier's bucket `quranic:root_000737:B011/m01`; bucket lug or balancing piece `quranic:root_000741:B008/m01`; dipper fastened to a long water-lifting pole `quranic:root_001491:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:7, 37:65 (ش ط ن); 37:8 (س م ع); 37:26, 37:79, 37:84, 37:103, 37:109, 37:120, 37:130, 37:181 (س ل م); 37:47 (ن ز ف); 37:137 (م ر ر)
- Synthesis: The apparatus makes extraction a coordinated upward action in which rope, lug, bucket, and pole share the load. It materializes the surah's recurrent rescue and provision language while sharpening the contrast with the garden spring and circulating cup, whose supply appears without this labor.

### S11. Stringing a Face-Turning Amulet
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Spacer beads and a charged face-turning bead are arranged into a short necklace intended to redirect one person's face toward another.
- Active motifs: spacer bead set between pearls `quranic:root_001159:B012/m01`; amulet bead meant to turn one face toward another `quranic:root_001198:B016/m01`; short necklace or collar `quranic:root_001231:B013/m01`; strung neck ornament `quranic:root_001294:B003/m01`

- Synthesis: The craft turns attraction into manufactured orientation: beads are spaced and strung so one charged piece is supposed to turn one face toward another. It usefully pressures the surah's false kinship and affiliation claims by contrasting manipulated attachment with divinely assigned relation and rank.

### S12. Hidden-Hand Riddle and Reveal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One participant poses an obscured challenge about what is concealed in a hand, another supplies an answer, and the hand is opened to settle it.
- Active motifs: riddling challenge that elicits a hidden answer `quranic:root_000478:B007/m01`; hand game of bringing out what is held `quranic:root_000400:B010/m01`; returning an answer in exchanged speech `quranic:root_000544:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:64 (خ ر ج); 37:68 (ر ج ع); 37:125 (د ع و)
- Synthesis: The game gives the surah's repeated questions a concrete disclosure rule: concealed content is challenged, brought out, and answered. It reframes polemical exchange as a revealable test rather than an indefinitely shifting assertion.

### S13. Gathering a Herd by Pipe
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A shepherd sounds a reed pipe and calls until grazing animals gather and move as a coordinated herd.
- Active motifs: shepherd's gathering call `quranic:root_000836:B006/m01`; shepherd's reed pipe `quranic:root_001586:B010/m01`; grazing livestock or camels `quranic:root_001525:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:43, 37:57, 37:75 (ن ع م); 37:70 (ه ر ع); 37:83 (ش ي ع)
- Synthesis: Pastoral sound becomes a technology of coordination: the pipe and call gather a scattered herd and set it moving together. This materializes the surah's opening ordered voices and its later summons, ranks, and driven collectives as acoustic alignment.

### S14. Contract, Procession, and Bridal Canopy
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A marriage is contracted, the bride is conveyed in procession, the pair is joined into a household, and entry takes place beneath an erected bridal canopy.
- Active motifs: marriage contract `quranic:root_001444:B004/m01`; taking a spouse and forming a household `quranic:root_000064:B002/m01`; conveying a bride in procession or palanquin `quranic:root_000635:B002/m01`; pairing spouses `quranic:root_000652:B003/m01`; erecting the bridal canopy and entering marriage `quranic:root_000156:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:22 (ز و ج); 37:76, 37:134 (ء ه ل); 37:94 (ز ف ف); 37:97, 37:102, 37:149, 37:153 (ب ن ي); 37:150 (م ل ك)
- Synthesis: The rite grounds pairing and household formation in a social sequence of contract, conveyance, pairing, and entry rather than in bare assertion. It therefore pressures the surah's disputed genealogy and gender attributions by giving legitimate relation a concrete form.

### S15. Camphor-Scented Burial Preparation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fragrance is prepared at a brazier, camphor supplies its scent, and the dead body is concealed in shroud or grave.
- Active motifs: perfume brazier `quranic:root_000973:B012/m01`; camphor fragrance `quranic:root_001307:B011/m01`; shrouding or concealing the dead `quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:22, 37:40, 37:74, 37:81, 37:85, 37:95, 37:111, 37:122, 37:128, 37:132, 37:160, 37:161, 37:169, 37:171 (ع ب د); 37:36, 37:43, 37:158 (ج ن ن); 37:170 (ك ف ر)
- Synthesis: Burial preparation combines fragrance with concealment: camphor and brazier accompany the body into shroud or grave. The scene materializes mortality as covered presence rather than erasure, sharpening the surah's insistence that those hidden in death can still be brought forth.

### S16. P1-P8 - Watering-Place Muster and Herd Yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Pericopes and bridge: P1, P2, P3, P4, P5, P6, P7, and P8; causal sequence and repeated scene signature: water-sustained encampment leads to allied muster, herd return and lining, shared watering, and eventual milk yield or scarcity.
- Scene or process: A traveling group settles where water can sustain it, coordinates access as herds return and line up, waters the animals at their mouths, and reads continuing milk or its absence as the outcome.
- Active motifs: water that enables settlement and livelihood `quranic:root_001444:B007/m01`; land suitable for a pleasant encampment `quranic:root_001525:B011/m01`; cooperation or alliance around a common undertaking `quranic:root_001441:B003/m01`; herd's return to water after grazing `quranic:root_000333:B003/m01`; lining up together at water `quranic:root_000871:B007/m01`; watering group sharing access with other herders `quranic:root_000783:B003/m01`; drawing water at camels' mouths `quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`; continuing abundant milk `quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`; camel milk becoming scarce or absent `quranic:root_000262:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:1, 37:165 (ص ف ف); 37:8 (ج ن ب); 37:8, 37:66 (م ل ء); 37:27, 37:44, 37:50, 37:71, 37:94 (ق ب ل); 37:37, 37:72, 37:123, 37:133, 37:139, 37:147, 37:171, 37:181 (ر س ل); 37:43, 37:57, 37:75 (ن ع م); 37:46 (ش ر ب); 37:57, 37:127, 37:158 (ح ض ر); 37:150 (م ل ك)
- Synthesis: The bridge makes collective order materially testable: alignment succeeds only when it coordinates access to the resource that permits camp, herd survival, and continuing yield. The surah's ranks, affiliations, and provision converge in a survival mechanism whose result becomes legible as abundant or empty udders.

### S17. P4-P5 - Inherited Report and Delivered Announcement
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P4 and P5; causal role progression and contrast: a report becomes inherited narration through transmission, while a delivered message arrives as consequential news whose authority depends on source and fulfillment.
- Scene or process: Speech is carried until it becomes a received report, then a message is delivered and enters the next pericope as consequential announcement.
- Active motifs: transmitting a report until it becomes inherited narration `quranic:root_000011:B002/m01`; delivering a message `quranic:root_000151:B002/m01`; consequential news brought to its recipient `quranic:root_001464:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:70 (ء ث ر); 37:102 (ب ل غ); 37:112 (ن ب ء)
- Synthesis: The P4-P5 bridge separates transmission from validation: repetition can make a report inherited, but the later announcement acquires authority through delivery and ensuing fulfillment. This pressures the contrast between ancestral precedent and revealed promise without treating circulation itself as proof.

### S18. Flaying, Tanning, and Patching a Hide
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hide is stripped and cleaned, treated with measured applications of tanning bark, inspected for perforation, and repaired by sewing thick edges together.
- Active motifs: flaying and extracting a hide `quranic:root_001476:B003/m01`; removing the outer skin or surface `quranic:root_000120:B004/m01`; tree bark or leaves used for tanning `quranic:root_000737:B008/m01`; one or two small applications of tanning material `quranic:root_001533:B007/m01`; mite-perforated or spoiled hide `quranic:root_000352:B004/m01`; thick hide edge `quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`; sewing hide to hide `quranic:root_000121:B006/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 37:26, 37:79, 37:84, 37:103, 37:109, 37:120, 37:130, 37:181 (س ل م); 37:76, 37:115, 37:134 (ن ج و); 37:101 (ح ل م); 37:101, 37:112 (ب ش ر); 37:113 (ن ف س); 37:175, 37:179 (ب ص ر)
- Synthesis: The craft materializes the surah's challenge to irreversible bodily disassembly: a removed surface can be preserved through transformation, damage remains diagnosable, and separated pieces can be joined into durable form.

### S19. Raising, Harvesting, and Gleaning a Date Palm
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Unnamed seedstock grows as a young palm, produces a crop, is harvested, and leaves fallen dates to be gleaned and spread before storage.
- Active motifs: seed-grown palm of unknown variety `quranic:root_000259:B011/m01`; young or short palm `quranic:root_000248:B006/m01`; emergence of palm yield `quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`; tree or palm crop `quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`; cutting fruit from the palm `quranic:root_000239:B001/m01`; fallen or dry dates remaining after harvest `quranic:root_000239:B002/m01`; dates spread before storage `quranic:root_001136:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:28, 37:117, 37:157 (ء ت ي); 37:34 (ج ر م); 37:63, 37:77, 37:98, 37:158 (ج ع ل); 37:66, 37:91 (ء ك ل); 37:107 (ف د ي); 37:134 (ج م ع)
- Synthesis: The sequence materializes blessed descent as productive continuity: unnamed seedstock becomes legible through actual yield, and even the harvest residue is preserved. It supports promised lineage while pressuring genealogical claims that produce no corresponding fruit.

### S20. Controlled Weaning and Its Failure Condition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A young animal is separated from nursing, with access blocked at both udder and mouth, while hunger or bodily harm marks excessive deprivation.
- Active motifs: offspring separated from mother or nursing `quranic:root_001159:B003/m01`; bag tied over a goat's udder to prevent suckling `quranic:root_000011:B012/m01`; disk or rod placed at a kid's tongue to prevent suckling `quranic:root_001177:B006/m01`; young animal harmed when nursing is withheld too severely `quranic:root_001116:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:21 (ف ص ل); 37:32 (غ و ي); 37:70 (ء ث ر); 37:140 (ف ل ك)
- Synthesis: Weaning reframes separation as a calibrated transition rather than successful restraint for its own sake: the procedure fails when it destroys the dependent life it is meant to mature. This usefully pressures the surah's ordeals and rescues by making preserved life, not deprivation alone, the operative outcome.

### S21. Feeding a Cistern and Apportioning Its Water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Distant runoff reaches a collection point, is directed through a watercourse into a leveled basin, and is distributed in known shares.
- Active motifs: distant ravines feeding a water collection `quranic:root_001510:B007/m01`; directing or clearing a watercourse to a basin `quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`; leveled cistern in which water remains `quranic:root_000109:B003/m01`; known allotted share of water `quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:25, 37:116, 37:172 (ن ص ر); 37:28, 37:117, 37:157 (ء ت ي); 37:46 (ش ر ب); 37:113 (ب ر ك)
- Synthesis: The mechanism materializes provision as governed flow: remote supply must be conducted, retained, and apportioned before it sustains a community. It supports the surah's provision and rescue scenes while pressing ownership claims with the prior dependence of possession on coordinated inflow and allocation.

### S22. Testing a Sword by Its Cut
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bright sword is handled by its hilt and kept in a close sheath, then its visible finish, iron quality, penetrating stroke, and resulting trace distinguish a hard cutter from a soft, ineffective blade.
- Active motifs: white or bright sword `quranic:root_000168:B018/m01`; sword hilt `quranic:root_000529:B005/m01`; slim sword enclosed in its sheath `quranic:root_001115:B008/m01`; hard or keen sword iron `quranic:root_000516:B002/m01`; soft non-cutting sword `quranic:root_000058:B002/m01`; cutting blade that penetrates in the stroke `quranic:root_000858:B003/m01`; blade luster, temper, or quality `quranic:root_000011:B007/m01`; stroke or its visible trace `quranic:root_000011:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 37:3, 37:13, 37:155, 37:168 (ذ ك ر); 37:23, 37:118 (ص ر ط); 37:46, 37:49 (ب ي ض); 37:47 (غ و ل); 37:65 (ر ء س); 37:70 (ء ث ر); 37:150 (ء ن ث)
- Synthesis: Brightness and finish do not establish efficacy; hardness becomes decisive only in a penetrating stroke and the trace it leaves. The test materializes the surah's blow against mute objects and its pledged victory as effects proved in action rather than by the display or name of force.

### S23. Claim, Surety, and Debt Collection
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A financial debt creates a specific enforceable right, a guarantor assumes liability through an undertaking, and the claimant follows the obligation through collection.
- Active motifs: financial debt or credit obligation `quranic:root_000504:B003/m01`; specific right owned against another `quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`; liability or assigned right that follows its holder `quranic:root_000186:B004/m01`; guarantor `quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`; written guarantee or undertaking `quranic:root_001198:B008/m02`; claimant demanding a right `quranic:root_000175:B005/m01`; collecting and satisfying the debt `quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 37:3 (ت ل و); 37:10 (ت ب ع); 37:20, 37:53 (د ي ن); 37:27, 37:44, 37:50, 37:71, 37:94 (ق ب ل); 37:31, 37:37 (ح ق ق); 37:39, 37:80, 37:105, 37:110, 37:121, 37:131 (ج ز ي)
- Synthesis: The surety sequence materializes allocated responsibility: debt gives one party a claim, and another bears it only through an actual undertaking before collection follows. This pressures the blame-shifting of followers and leaders by distinguishing asserted influence from a binding assumption of liability.
